# CELL_LINE

## Technical definition

Name of the perturbed cell line or cell population as given by ORCS. Joined to Cellosaurus for standard identity (accession, category, disease, origin).

## Plain-language description

Which cells received the gene changes. Most are 'cell lines': cells first taken from a patient or animal and then grown in labs for many years.

## Where it goes in DeathMap

Screens.cell_line_reported; Screens.perturbed_population_reported; Screens.disease_context_normalized (candidate)


## Summary by Cellosaurus category

| Category | Distinct cell lines |
|---|---:|
| Cancer cell line | 730 |
| no Cellosaurus category | 54 |
| Transformed cell line | 18 |
| Spontaneously immortalized cell line | 7 |
| Embryonic stem cell | 4 |
| Factor-dependent cell line | 3 |
| Telomerase immortalized cell line | 2 |
| Induced pluripotent stem cell | 2 |
| Somatic stem cell | 2 |
| Finite cell line | 1 |
| Hybridoma | 1 |
| Hybrid cell line | 1 |

## Flags

- `immune-role`: a cancer-derived or transformed line that is often used as an immune cell (for example Jurkat as a T cell, RAW 264.7 or J774 as macrophages). Its Cellosaurus category says nothing about its role in a given screen, so check whether it was the perturbed target or the effector.
- `TNBC model`: commonly used triple-negative breast cancer model. Hand-curated list; confirm against Cellosaurus comments and the publication.
- `review`: the saved Cellosaurus annotation recorded a caution, misidentification, or unresolved match (full text in the Review column).

It is dubious that this cell line originates from an African American 47 year old patient diagnosed with an ovarian clear cell adenocarcinoma. Immunohistochemical and qPCR analysis as well as its mutational profile support an ovarian serous adenocarcinoma origin (PubMed=25049276; DOI=10.26502/ogr072).
